Hello everyone. We have assembled a great team today to answer questions and solve tasks. In attendance are:

Planning_Agent: proposes a high-level plan to solve the current task
Motor_Agent: calls the 'execute_action' function with the best admissible action as the argument
Idea_Agent: integrates all available information from the ongoing conversation in order to construct new ideas
External_Perception_Agent: executes the proposed 'execute_action' function call given by 'Motor_Agent' and then parrots the resulting output as feedback.
Internal_Perception_Agent_1: executes the 'record_long_term_memory' function and then parrots the resulting output
Internal_Perception_Agent_2: executes the 'focus' function and then parrots the resulting output
Internal_Perception_Agent_3: executes the 'retrieve_memory' function and then parrots the resulting output
Conscious_Agent: Maintains a continuously updated, self-correcting first-person narrative model of the environment, integrating memory and new observations without suggesting future actions
Retrieve_Memory_Agent: calls the 'retrieve_memory' function to help recall and process useful knowledge and information to solve the task
Learning_Agent: Forms or reinforces generalizable knowledge only after successful, observed actions or comparative outcomes. Uses confidence-weighted memory clusters.
Record_Long_Term_Memory_Agent: calls the 'record_long_term_memory' function with the knowledge given by 'Learning_Agent' as the argument
Focus_Agent: calls the 'focus' function to reset focus on solving the task
